Supports both continuous and discrete action spaces.\\n- **meta_learning_env.py**: Environment wrapper for training the meta-learning controller. algorithm you think is suitable\\n\\n**You can also try:**\\n- Hybrid algorithms combining multiple approaches\\n- Multi-population / island model with migration\\n- Archive-based selection mechanisms\\n- Different initialization strategies, boundary handling, diversity maintenance\\n\\n**Meta-Learning Layer:**\\n- Try different neural network architectures (deeper, wider, different activations)\\n- Change the observation space (what information the controller sees)\\n- Change the action space (what parameters the controller outputs)\\n- Try different training strategies (reward algorithm, ensure `evals` is incremented correctly for every function evaluation\\n\\nNOTE: The scoring is handled externally. return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = else:\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n                \n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n                \n initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n This is\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n\"\"\"\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    # Set global seed at module load time\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\n\nclass ParamController(ABC):\n    @abstractmethod\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n        self.params = dict(params)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        return dict(self.params)\n\n\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n        self.w_start = w_start\n        self.w_end = w_end\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}---META---\n\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n\nProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\nThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\nDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n self.done = False\n\n    def reset(self, problem, max_evals: int, init_x: Optional[list] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Attach a cocoex `problem` and initialize internal state.\n\n        Returns initial observation.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.problem = problem\n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.done = False\n        if init_x is None:\n            try:\n                init_x = problem.initial_solution\n            except Exception:\n                init_x = [0] * problem.dimension\n        self.current_best_x = list(init_x)\n        f = float(problem(self.current_best_x))\n        self.current_best_f = f\n        self.init_best_f = f\n        self.total_evals = problem.evaluations\n        return self._get_obs()\n\n    def step(self, action: Any) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], float, bool, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Apply action (dict or array-like) as DE params, run DE for a\n        short `step_budget` evaluations starting from current best, and return\n        observation, reward, done, info.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.done:\n            return self._get_obs(), 0.0, True, {}\n\n        # normalize action into params dict\n        params = {}\n        if isinstance(action, dict):\n            params.update(action)\n        else:\n            # assume array-like [F, CR]\n            params['F'] = float(action[0])\n            params['CR'] = float(action[1])\n\n        # run a short DE search\n        de = DEOptimizer(self.problem.dimension, getattr(self.problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(self.problem, 'upper_bounds', None), self.step_budget, params=params, swarm_size=self.pop_size)\n        xopt, stats = de.optimize(self.problem, x0=self.current_best_x)\n\n        new_best = stats.get('gbest_f', float('inf'))\n        new_x = stats.get('gbest', None)\n\n        # reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n        reward = 0.0\n        if new_best < self.current_best_f:\n            reward = 1.0\n            self.current_best_f = new_best\n            if new_x is not None:\n                self.current_best_x = list(new_x)\n\n        # update eval counters\n        self.total_evals = stats.get('evals', self.total_evals)\n        done = (self.total_evals >= self.max_evals) or getattr(self.problem, 'final_target_hit', False)\n        self.done = done\n\n        obs = self._get_obs()\n        info = {'de_stats': stats}\n        return obs, float(reward), done, info\n\n    def _get_obs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        return {'iteration': 0, 'evals': int(self.total_evals), 'gbest_f': float(self.current_best_f), 'initial_gbest_f': float(self.init_best_f), 'max_evals': int(self.max_evals)}\n\n    def render(self):\n        print('evals', self.total_evals, 'best_f', self.current_best_f)\"\n    }\n    </json>",
    "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Make sure to include enough context in `old_str` to make it unique\\n* The `new_str` parameter should contain the edited lines that should replace the `old_str`', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'enum': ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert', 'undo_edit'], 'description': 'The commands to run. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`, `undo_edit`.'}, 'file_text': {'description': 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.', 'type': 'string'}, 'insert_line': {'description': 'Required parameter of `insert` command. The `new_str` will be inserted AFTER the line `insert_line` of `path`.', 'type': 'integer'}, 'new_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the new string. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '1,260p' /hada/metabbo/param_controller.py && echo '---META---' && sed -n '1,220p' /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py && echo '---ENV---' && sed -n '1,200p' /hada/metabbo/meta_learning_env.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"\"\"\"Parameter controller interface and simple implementations.\n\nDefines `ParamController` abstract interface and example controllers that can\nbe injected into `PSOOptimizer` to adjust parameters dynamically. This is\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n\"\"\"\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    # Set global seed at module load time\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\n\nclass ParamController(ABC):\n    @abstractmethod\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n        self.params = dict(params)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        return dict(self.params)\n\n\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n        self.w_start = w_start\n        self.w_end = w_end\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}---META---\n\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n\nProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\nThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\nDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n---ENV---\n\"\"\"Environment wrapper to train DQN to control DE parameters.\n\nImplements a tiny OpenAI Gym-like interface: reset(problem, max_evals, init_x),\nstep(action) -> (obs, reward, done, info).\n\nReward convention: 1.0 if a strictly better solution is found during the\nstep, 0.0 otherwise.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Tuple, Optional\n\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\nthis_dir = os.path.dirname(__file__)\ndef _load_local(name, filename):\n    path = os.path.join(this_dir, filename)\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)\n    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n    return module\n\n# load local ec_algorithm module\n_ec_mod = _load_local('cocoex_example.ec_algorithm', 'ec_algorithm.py')\nDEOptimizer = _ec_mod.DEOptimizer\n\n\nclass DQNEnv:\n    def __init__(self, step_budget: int = 20, pop_size: int = None):\n        self.problem = None\n        self.max_evals = 0\n        self.step_budget = int(step_budget)\n        self.pop_size = pop_size\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.current_best_x = None\n        self.current_best_f = float('inf')\n        self.done = False\n\n    def reset(self, problem, max_evals: int, init_x: Optional[list] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Attach a cocoex `problem` and initialize internal state.\n\n        Returns initial observation.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.problem = problem\n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.done = False\n        if init_x is None:\n            try:\n                init_x = problem.initial_solution\n            except Exception:\n                init_x = [0] * problem.dimension\n        self.current_best_x = list(init_x)\n        f = float(problem(self.current_best_x))\n        self.current_best_f = f\n        self.init_best_f = f\n        self.total_evals = problem.evaluations\n        return self._get_obs()\n\n    def step(self, action: Any) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], float, bool, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Apply action (dict or array-like) as DE params, run DE for a\n        short `step_budget` evaluations starting from current best, and return\n        observation, reward, done, info.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.done:\n            return self._get_obs(), 0.0, True, {}\n\n        # normalize action into params dict\n        params = {}\n        if isinstance(action, dict):\n            params.update(action)\n        else:\n            # assume array-like [F, CR]\n            params['F'] = float(action[0])\n            params['CR'] = float(action[1])\n\n        # run a short DE search\n        de = DEOptimizer(self.problem.dimension, getattr(self.problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(self.problem, 'upper_bounds', None), self.step_budget, params=params, swarm_size=self.pop_size)\n        xopt, stats = de.optimize(self.problem, x0=self.current_best_x)\n\n        new_best = stats.get('gbest_f', float('inf'))\n        new_x = stats.get('gbest', None)\n\n        # reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n        reward = 0.0\n        if new_best < self.current_best_f:\n            reward = 1.0\n            self.current_best_f = new_best\n            if new_x is not None:\n                self.current_best_x = list(new_x)\n\n        # update eval counters\n        self.total_evals = stats.get('evals', self.total_evals)\n        done = (self.total_evals >= self.max_evals) or getattr(self.problem, 'final_target_hit', False)\n        self.done = done\n\n        obs = self._get_obs()\n        info = {'de_stats': stats}\n        return obs, float(reward), done, info\n\n    def _get_obs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        return {'iteration': 0, 'evals': int(self.total_evals), 'gbest_f': float(self.current_best_f), 'initial_gbest_f': float(self.init_best_f), 'max_evals': int(self.max_evals)}\n\n    def render(self):\n        print('evals', self.total_evals, 'best_f', self.current_best_f)\"\n    }\n    </json>"

erate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_9/bbob_unconstrained_eval/predictions.csv\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_9/bbob_unconstrained_eval/report.json\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/bbob_unconstrained_loss_curves.png\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/bbob_unconstrained_return_curves.png\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/generate.log\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/predictions.csv\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/report.json\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/progress_info_bbob_unconstrained_train_agent.txt\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/progress_plot_bbob_unconstrained_train_agent.png\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dataset.csv\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_pso_util.py\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\n---\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n            'controller': controller\n        }\n    \n    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n        'score': score,\n        'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n        'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n        'opt_val': opt_val,\n        'training_loss': None,\n        'sweep_return': controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None,\n        'controller': controller\n    }\"\n    }\n    </json>",

erate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_9/bbob_unconstrained_eval/predictions.csv\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_9/bbob_unconstrained_eval/report.json\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/bbob_unconstrained_loss_curves.png\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/bbob_unconstrained_return_curves.png\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/generate.log\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/predictions.csv\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_initial/bbob_unconstrained_eval/report.json\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/progress_info_bbob_unconstrained_train_agent.txt\n/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/progress_plot_bbob_unconstrained_train_agent.png\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dataset.csv\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_pso_util.py\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\n---\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n            done\n        )\n\n        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n        else:\n and reset counter\n        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\", 'new_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population: use x0 if provided, otherwise uniform random\\n        if x0 is not None:\\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).reshape(1, -1)\\n            if x0_arr.shape[1] != self.dim:\\n                x0_arr = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (1, self.dim))\\n            x0_arr = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\\n            pop = np.empty((pop_size, self.dim))\\n            pop[0] = x0_arr[0]\\n            if pop_size > 1:\\n                pop[1:] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size - 1, self.dim))\\n                # Add small local perturbations around x0 to exploit the seed\\n                n_pert = min(pop_size - 1, 2)\\n                pert = 0.05 * (ub - lb) * rng.standard_normal((n_pert, self.dim))\\n                pop[1:1 + n_pert] += pert\\n                pop[1:] = np.clip(pop[1:], lb, ub)\\n        else:\\n            pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # JADE archive and parameter memories\\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\\n        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, int(0.5 * pop_size))\\n        mu_F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\\n        mu_CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\\n        p_best_rate = 0.15\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # If a controller supplies F/CR, use them as generation-level means\\n            if 'F' in params:\\n                mu_F = float(params['F'])\\n            if 'CR' in params:\\n                mu_CR = float(params['CR'])\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Sort by fitness for pbest selection\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            pbest_pool = order[:n_pbest]\\n            \\n            old_pop = pop.copy()\\n            trials = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE current-to-pbest/1\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # pbest individual\\n                pbest_idx = int(pbest_pool[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                \\n                # Choose two distinct vectors, not i\\n                available = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(available) < 2:\\n                    r1, r2 = 0, 1 if i != 1 else 2\\n                else:\\n                    r1, r2 = rng.choice(available, size=2, replace=False)\\n                \\n                # Sample F (Cauchy) and CR (normal) around memories\\n                F_i = mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(1.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\\n                \\n                # Optional archive use: sometimes pick r2 from the archive\\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    r2_arch = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\\n                    base2 = archive[r2_arch]\\n                else:\\n                    base2 = pop[r2]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - base2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover (ensure at least one component from mutant)\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip and re-scatter a small fraction near bounds\\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\\n                trials[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate all trials (simultaneous selection)\\n            new_pop = pop.copy()\\n            new_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n            replaced = np.zeros(pop_size, dtype=bool)\\n            successful_F = []\\n            successful_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trials[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    new_pop[i] = trials[i].copy()\\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    replaced[i] = True\\n                    successful_F.append(F_vals[i])\\n                    successful_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trials[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Apply selection\\n            pop = new_pop\\n            fitness = new_fitness\\n            \\n            # Update archive with replaced parents\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if replaced[i]:\\n                    parent = old_pop[i]\\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_capacity:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, parent.reshape(1, -1)])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))] = parent\\n            \\n            # Update parameter memories with successful trials\\n            if successful_F:\\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(successful_F))\\n                if sum_F > 0.0:\\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(np.square(successful_F)) / sum_F)\\n            if successful_CR:\\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(successful_CR))\\n            \\n            # Slowly adapt pbest proportion: more exploration early, focus later\\n            p_best_rate = max(0.05, min(0.25, 0.18 - 0.10 * min(1.0, iteration / 50.0)))\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"The file /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py has been edited. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file.
